Org. Setting and Reporting
This post is located in the Press and External Relations Section of the UN Information Service in Geneva, Strategic Communications Division of the Department of Global Communications. The Press and External Relations Section is responsible for promoting the work and mission of the United Nations through a range of media-related activities, the development of effective relations with key stakeholders and partners, and outreach activities designed to inform and engage with target audiences, in particular youth. The Associate Public Information Officer reports to the Chief of Press and External Relations.

Responsibilities
Within delegated authority, the Associate Public Information Officer may be responsible for the following duties: 

Tracks, researches and analyzes information on assigned topics/issues; gathers information from diverse sources and helps to assess news value and other potential impact, as well as to evaluate the effectiveness of information campaigns.
Drafts/compiles a specific type or types (e.g. print, broadcast, etc.), of information communications products for target audiences, to include press releases, media packets and reports, brochures, briefings, video clips, newsletters, websites, etc.
Organizes the clearance, production and distribution of information material; conducts photo and graphic research.
Prepares, on the basis of official UN documentation and other sources, initials drafts of articles or chapters for inclusion in UN newsletters, periodicals, reports and books.
In consultation with others, identifies and proposes information opportunities, activities and approaches, taking into account the situation/topic and target audience.
Identifies key contacts/constituencies and opportunities for strategic partnerships to facilitate communication efforts and maintains working relationships with the same.
Organizes or participates in the organization of conferences, seminar, press briefings, interviews, etc.; prepares briefing materials for senior officials prior to their participation in such events.
Responds to a variety of inquires and information requests internally and externally; prepares related correspondence.
Contributes to the collection and analysis of data as well as identification of trends or patterns and provides draft insights through graphs, charts, tables and reports using data visualization methods for data-driven planning, decision-making, presentation and reporting.
Produces short, engaging explainer videos for social media to communicate United Nations issues and activities in a clear and accessible way.
Performs other duties as assigned. 
Competencies
Professionalism: 

Ability to plan, execute and monitor public communication campaigns, e.g. campaign management, market research, message targeting, impact evaluation. Ability to research and analyze issues and topics related to current events. Ability to draft and compile a variety of written communication products in a clear, concise style. Ability to interact with the public to build and maintain effective business connections. 
-Shows pride in work and in achievements. 
-Demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter. Is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results. Is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns. 
-Shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. [Add gender indicator here].
-Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work.

Communication: 
-Speaks and writes clearly and effectively. 
-Listens to others, correctly interprets messages from others and responds appropriately. 
-Asks questions to clarify, and exhibits interest in having two-way communication. 
-Tailors language, tone, style and format to match the audience. 
-Demonstrates openness in sharing information and keeping people informed.

Planning and organizing: 
-Develops clear goals that are consistent with agreed strategies. 
-Identifies priority activities and assignments; adjusts priorities as required. 
-Allocates appropriate amount of time and resources for completing work. 
-Foresees risks and allows for contingencies when planning. 
-Monitors and adjusts plans and actions as necessary. 
-Uses time efficiently.

 

Eligibility & Suitability Req

Org. Setting and Reporting
This position is located in the Inspection and Evaluation Division (IED) of the Office of Internal Oversight Services (OIOS) at the United Nations Headquarters in New York. The Associate Evaluation Officer will report to a designated Chief of Section. Inspection and evaluation assignments are undertaken in all areas of work of the United Nations Secretariat, i.e., in the maintenance of international peace and security, promotion of sustainable development, delivery of humanitarian aid, protection of human rights, upholding of international laws, as well as in management support services. The incumbent will report to a Chief of Section and to the Director of OIOS/IED.

The Office of Internal Oversight Services is an independent office reporting to the SecretaryGeneral and to the General Assembly. The Inspection and Evaluation Division (IED) focuses on the conduct of independent inspections and evaluations on behalf of the Secretary-General and the Member States. IED evaluations and inspections are meant to assist intergovernmental bodies and programme managers in assessing the relevance, efficiency, effectiveness and impact of Secretariat programmes. In Accordance with its mandate, set forth in General Assembly resolution A/RES/48/218 B (and later resolutions A/RES/54/244 and A/RES/59/272), the role of IED is to help assure the accountability of Secretariat programmes for attaining their mandates, while in the process foster institutional learning and improvement through reflection by programmes and Member States on performance and results.

Responsibilities
The Associate Evaluation Officer will be responsible for the following duties:

• Assist the evaluation project leader with the planning, development and conduct of assigned projects, such as in-depth evaluations of complex programmes, thematic evaluations and ad hoc evaluations or equivalent studies
• Carry out, under supervision, all the tasks involved in the assigned evaluations, conducting data collection and analysis
• Assist in drafting reports, monitoring project development and implementation
• Review the results of projects, relevant documents and reports
• Identify problems and issues to be addressed and recommend corrective actions; liaise with relevant parties; identify and track follow-up actions
• Assist in policy development based on evaluation findings concerning programme relevance, effectiveness, efficiency and impact
• Undertake evaluation outreach activities
• Assist in developing and conducting training workshops, seminars, etc. to support evaluation, programme monitoring and management review exercises done by other departments; make presentations on assigned topics/activities
• Provide substantive support to consultative and other meetings, conferences, etc. Contribute to efforts to strengthen the role of evaluation in the United Nations Organization so as to improve programme efficiency and effectiveness
• The Associate Evaluation Officer may, as required, provide support to the Division on Division-wide operational or administrative matters, i.e., work planning, risk assessment, workplan monitoring, Division-wide change initiatives, human resource management, budget management, and general administrative matters, functioning as part of the Division

Competencies
PROFESSIONALISM: Knowledge and understanding of theories, concepts and approaches relevant to particular sector, functional area, or other specialized field. Ability to identify issues, analyse and participate in the resolution of issues/problems. Ability to assist with data collection using various methods. Conceptual analytical and evaluative skills to conduct independent research and analysis, including familiarity with and experience in the use of various research sources, including electronic sources on the Internet, intranet and other databases. Ability to apply judgment in the context of assignments given, plan own work and manage conflicting priorities. Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work.

TEAMWORK: Works collaboratively with colleagues to achieve Organizational goals; solicits input by genuinely valuing others' ideas and expertise; is willing to learn from others; places team agenda before personal agenda; supports and acts in accordance with final group decision, even when such decisions may not entirely reflect own position; shares credit for team accomplishments and accepts joint responsibility for team shortcomings.

PLANNING AND ORGANIZING: Develops clear goals that are consistent with agreed strategies; identifies priority activities and assignments; adjusts priorities as required; allocates appropriate amount of time and resources for completing work; foresees risks and allows for contingencies when planning; monitors and adjusts plans and actions as necessary; uses time efficiently.

Eligibility & Suitability Req
Education:
A first-level university degree is required; preferably in programme evaluation, social science, public administration, business administration, economics, or related field.

Work experience:
A minimum of two years of progressively responsible experience in project/programme management, programme evaluation, administration, or a related area is desirable.

Language:
English and French are the two working languages of the United Nations. For this post, fluency in English is required. Knowledge of another UN official language is an advantage.

Org. Setting and Reporting
The United Nations Office at Nairobi (UNON) is the UN headquarters in Africa and the respective office of the Secretary-General. UNON supports programme implementation of the UN Environment Programme (UNEP), the UN Human Settlements Programme (UN-Habitat), the Resident Coordinator System, the International Residual Mechanism for Criminal Tribunals (IRMCT), as well as other UN offices in Kenya, by providing administrative, conference and information services www.unon.org).

 

This post is located in the office of Budget and Financial Management Services (BFMS), Division of Administrative Services (DAS) at the Nairobi duty station. Under the direct supervision of the Chief of Unit, the incumbent is responsible for the following duties:

Responsibilities
Accounting:

Responsible for the processing of financial documents in line with the financial rules and regulations.
Review and analysis of various accounting data, financial statements input and preparation of reports.
Scrutinize supporting documents for completeness, accuracy and validity of requests in line with applicable policies, procedures, rules and regulations.
Verify accuracy of postings and process corrections as appropriate.
Analyze and monitor suspense/open item managed accounts (OIM) in the ERP system (Umoja) and initiate and coordinate corrective action.
Perform approval functions of financial transactions as per delegation of authority.
Perform quality review of reports before issuance, prepare donor reports and financial statements.
Monitor cash balances, forecast cash needs and coordinate timely funding.
Process cash payments and electronic disbursements, maintain records and reconcile cash journals and maintain cash reports for monthly and year-end financial statement reporting.
Budget preparation:

Review and analyze programme budget/financial implications to the Security Council statements in light of additional mandates.
Assist managers in the elaboration of resource requirements for budget submissions.
Provide support to relevant intergovernmental and expert bodies in their budget review; assists in securing approval from the General Assembly.
Budget administration:

Monitor budget implementation and recommend reallocation of funds as necessary.
Monitor expenditures to ensure that they remain within authorized levels.
Administer and monitor extra-budgetary resources, including review of agreements and cost plans, ensuring compliance with regulations and rules and established policies and procedures.
Assist in the preparation of budget performance submissions.
Review and assist in finalization of budget performance reports, analyzing variances between approved budgets and actual expenditures.
Assist in providing substantive support to intergovernmental and expert bodies.
General:

Keep up to date on documents/reports/guidelines that have a bearing on matters related to programme budgets, ensuring compliance with intergovernmental recommendations and decisions as well as with United Nations policies and procedures;
Keep up to date accounting desktop and standard operating procedures to align with accounting standards, UN policies and procedures
Perform other related duties, as assigned.
Competencies
PROFESSIONALISM: Ability to identify and resolve budgetary problems and identify sources for data collection. Shows pride in work and in achievements. Demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter. Is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results. Is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns. Shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges. Remains calm in stressful situations. Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work. 

ACCOUNTABILITY: Takes ownership of all responsibilities and honours commitments. Delivers outputs for which one has responsibility within prescribed time, cost and quality standards. Operates in compliance with organizational regulations and rules. Supports subordinates, provides oversight and takes responsibility for delegated assignments. Takes personal responsibility for his/her own shortcomings and those of the work unit, where applicable. 

CLIENT ORIENTATION: Considers all those to whom services are provided to be "clients" and seeks to see things from clients' point of view. Establishes and maintains productive partnerships with clients by gaining their trust and respect. Identifies clients' needs and matches them to appropriate solutions. Monitors ongoing developments inside and outside the clients' environment to keep informed and anticipate problems. Keeps client informed of progress or or setbacks in projects. Meets timeline for delivery of products or services to client.

Eligibility & Suitability Req
Education:
A first-level university degree is required, preferably in business administration, finance or related field.

Work Experience:

Experience in finance, budget or related area is desirable. Working experience with the Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) is desirable.

Candidates for this position must have passed the United Nations Young Professional Programme Examination (YPP), or the General Service to Professional Examination (G to P).

Org. Setting and Reporting
This position is located in the Recruitment Services Unit (RSU) in the Human Resources Management Section (HRMS) of the Administration and Conference Management Division (ACMD) at the United Nations Economic Commission for Africa (UNECA).   The post encompasses such sub-functional specialties as recruitment and placement of both staff and affiliate personnel, administration of affiliate personnel, human resources planning, and staff development and career support in areas relevant to its functions. The Associate Human Resources Officer reports directly to the Chief of the Unit and is under overall supervision by the Chief of Section, Human Resources.

Responsibilities
Within delegated authority, the Associate Human Resources Officer will be responsible for the following duties: 

General

Provides advice and support to managers and staff on human resources related matters within the post’s purview. 
Keeps abreast of developments in various areas of human resources.
Recruitment and placement

Coordinates with client offices in identifying upcoming vacancies.
Prepares vacancy announcements, reviews applications, and provides a short-list to clients’ offices.
Reviews recommendation on the selection of candidate by client offices. 
Prepares and presents cases to central review bodies or monitors the submissions by Human Resources Assistants .
Serves as ex-officio in examinations boards and interviews. 
Monitors the work of the Human Resources Assistants in carrying out  human resources actions in the area of recruitment of staff and recruitment and administration of affiliate personnel including preparation of personnel actions, maintenance of the HR MiniMasters, and processing of contracts.
Supervises the maintenance of the human resources filing systems within the post’s purview .
Assists in planning, organizing, developing and tests related to recruitment of international and national professional, general service categories of staff and affiliate personnel.
Organizes and coordinates the ECA  Internship and Fellowship Programmes.
Administration of entitlements

Provides advice on salary and related benefits, travel, and social security entitlements at the recruitment stage for staff and on provisions related to affiliate personnel at all stages of their engagement.
Provides advice on interpretation and application of policies, regulations and rules. Reviews and provides advice on exceptions to policies, regulations and rules.
Reviews the applications for internship programmes; handles correspondence with applicants.
Staff development and career support training 

Identifies and analyzes staff development and career support needs and designs programmes to meet identified needs within the post’s purview.
Provides induction orientation and briefing to new staff members and affiliate personnel if necessary to respond to additional questions.
Other duties

Prepares classification analysis of jobs in Professional and General Service and related categories and acts as a classification officer for posts in the General Service category.
Provides guidance to programme managers on the application of classification policies and procedures and by undertaking whole office review. 
Contributes to the collection and analysis of data as well as identification of trends or patterns and provides draft insights through graphs, charts, tables and reports using data visualization methods for data-driven planning, decision-making, presentation and reporting, as well as to data collection and gathering business requirements across departments to identify pain points and support data-driven analysis. 
Compiles and clarifies data insights and develops supporting materials in preparation for stakeholder communications in the context of UN2.0.
Competencies
Professionalism: Knowledge of human resources policies, practices and procedures and ability to apply them in an organizational setting. Ability to identify issues, formulate opinions, make conclusions and recommendations. Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work. Able to analyze and interpret data in support of decision-making and convey resulting information to management.

Planning& Organizing:  Develops clear goals that are consistent with agreed strategies; identifies priority activities and assignments; adjusts priorities as required; allocates appropriate amount of time and resources for completing work; foresees risks and allows for contingencies when planning; monitors and adjusts plans and actions as necessary; uses time efficiently.

Client Orientation: Considers all those to whom services are provided to be “clients” and seeks to see things from clients’ point of view; establishes and maintains productive partnerships with clients by gaining their trust and respect; identifies clients’ needs and matches them to appropriate solutions; monitors ongoing developments inside and outside the clients’ environment to keep informed and anticipate problems; keeps clients informed of progress or setbacks in projects; meets timeline for delivery of products or services to client.

Eligibility & Suitability Req
Education:  

A first-level university degree is required; preferably in human resources management, business or public

administration, social sciences, education or related field is desirable. 

 

Experience: 

Experience in human resources management, administration or related area is desirable.  

Experience in data analytics or related area is desirable

Org. Setting and Reporting
The incumbent supports the Scheduling Office of the Secretary-General.  He/She utilizes political, communications, planning and coordination skills to support the functions related to the Unit.

The EOSG, under the guidance of the Secretary-General (SG), work as a team pursuing in a cohesive and coherent manner, the SG's vision, priorties and policies for his second mandate in office.  It seeks alignment of the United Nations family with the SG's vision, priorities and policies.  It is guided by the necessity of securing the highest standards of efficiency, competence and integrity and of achieving gender and regional balance among staff.

 All EOSG units will support the EOSG senior officials, as appropriate, depending on the tasks at hand, regardless of which senior official they formally report to, and will work together through a matrix team approach, as necessary, to ensure EOSG leads by example on integration and cross-pillar work.

Responsibilities
The incumbent is responsible for the following duties and workplan:

Coordinates the request(s) of briefing materials on substantive issues from relevant lead UN Departments/Offices.
Assists senior staff of the Scheduling Unit in daily monitoring of incoming correspondence and requests for appropriate dispatching to the substantive units and identifies actions to be taken with the UN Global Secretariat and relevant concerned Departments/Offices.
Channel recommendations into the Secretary-General's daily briefing note for the decision of the Director of Office and Secretary-General.
Assists senior staff with the preparation of briefing notes, political background information and drafting of letters.
Drafts and/or coordinates the drafting of routine replies, memorandums and responses to correspondences in compliance with the UN Correspondence Manual and Editorial Manual and UN Protocol.
Supports the coordination of the Secretary-General's briefing material for bilateral meetings and its clearance with EOSG Units.
Ensure timely delivery of briefing materials to EOSG Managers and Secretary-General Team for final clearance.
Participates in operational planning and coordinates and seeks advice from EOSG Units and substantive Departments in the Secretariat regarding priorities to be assigned to each request, in order to enable the Scheduling Office to make recommendations to the Secretary-General travel programme.
Acts as a focal point about letters the Office receives and suggests next action.
In particular, keeps abreast of all information about political or situational issues in the world.
Others:

Contributes to the re-organizing the share-drive and keeping track of monthly and yearly statistics related to the engagements undertaken by the Secretary-General such as the number of trips, phone calls, engagements, bilateral meetings and public occasions.
Supports the Director and Senior Officials in the Scheduling Office on the planning and arrangements related to the Secretary-General's high-level daily engagements with Heads of Delegation and during the General Assembly.  This involves compiling requests, seeking advice from EOSG Units and substantive Departments in the global Secretariat regarding priorities to be assigned to each request as well as preparing high, medium and low-priority tables for decision and approval of the Director and support other organizational tasking as identified by Senior Officers.
 

Competencies
Professionalism:  Ability to identify and analyze political, ethnic, racial, social and economic problems that cause civil unrest in a country or geographic area.  Knowledge of technical issues surrounding elections.  Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments; observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations.  Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work.  Able to analyze and interpret data in support of decision-making and convey resulting information to management.

Communication:  Speaks and writes clearly and effectively; listens to others, correctly interprets messages from others and responds appropriately; asks questions to clarify, and exhibits interest in having two-way communication; tailor language. tone, style and format to match audience; demonstrates openness in sharing information and keeping people informed.

Planning and Organizing:  Develops clear goals that are consistent with agreed strategies; identifies priority activities and assignments; adjusts priorities as required; allocates appropriate amount of time and resources for completing work; foresees risks and allows for contingencies when planning; monitors and adjusts plans and actions as necessary; uses time efficiently.

Eligibility & Suitability Req
Education: A first-level university degree is required, preferably in political science, international relations, international economics, law, public administration, or related field is desirable. Successful completion of both degree and non-degree programs in data analytics, business analytics or data science programs will be an advantage.

Experience: A minimum of three years of progressively responsible experience in political science. international relations, law, disarmament, security, development management, conflict resolution or related area is desirable.  Two years or more experience in data analytics or related area is desirable.

Language:  English and French are the two working languages of the United Nations.  For this post, fluency in English is required.  Knowledge of another UN official language is an advantage.

Org. Setting and Reporting
This position is located in the Department of Economic and Social Affairs (DESA)in the Division for Social Development.  The Associate Social Affairs Officer reports to the senior Social Affairs Officer. 

 

Responsibilities
Within delegated authority, the Associate Social Affairs Officer will be responsible for the following duties: 


Social Analysis

Carries out basic research on selected aspects of critical areas of concern related to socio-economic issues, social development programmes, etc., to include collecting, analyzing and presenting statistical data and other information gathered from diverse sources, participating in the analysis of national experiences in social planning and policy development, etc., assists in developing and maintaining datasets on socio-economic issues and trends;
Contributes to the preparation of various written outputs, e.g. draft background papers, sections of reports and studies, inputs to publications on selected social issues, including inequality, poverty, labour market issues and social inclusion; 
Develops and maintains reference/resource information on specific topics, policy-related issues, recent developments and trends in the field, etc.; liaises with NGOs, researchers, etc. to obtain and provide data and information; responds to various inquiries and information requests internally and externally. 
Provides administrative and substantive support to expert group meetings, panel discussions, conferences, etc., to include proposing agenda topics, researching and proposing experts, handling logistics, etc.
Analyzes progress and developments in the implementation of specific programmes; contributes to the development of mechanisms for monitoring programme implementation.
Undertakes outreach activity in assigned areas, providing presentations and other inputs (e.g. articles, publications), and attending professional,NGO and other meetings.
Maintains awareness of current social affairs and related issues, to include relevant political, policy, gender issues/considerations or other developments in specific sector, country or region concerned.
 

Intergovernmental Support

Provides support to intergovernmental processes dealing with social development issues by: preparing inputs for reports to intergovernmental bodies (Commission for Social Development, Conference of States Parties to the Convention on the Rights of Persons with Disabilities, Permanent Forum on Indigenous Issues, General Assembly); following intergovernmental meetings and preparing summary reports and similar material; preparing inputs to statements by members of the Bureau and Secretariat staff to such meetings; assisting in the organization of panels, round tables, etc. on social development issues for intergovernmental processes and other meetings.
 

Technical Cooperation

Compiles and analyses the data and other information necessary for the preparation of technical cooperation project proposals in the area of social development.
Prepares documentation for technical cooperation programmes and projects.
Contributes to the preparation of material for the approval of regional, country or sectoral technical cooperation projects.
Monitors, backstops and assesses the implementation of technical cooperation programmes and projects.
Participates in fact-finding and advisory missions to government and other activities in support of work programme.
 

General

Performs other related duties as required, including a variety of administrative tasks necessary for the final delivery of the work unit’s services, including the preparation of background notes and talking points for senior mangement.
Undertakes on-the-job and other training activities, both internally and externally 
Contributes to the collection and analysis of data as well as identification of trends or patterns and provides draft insights through graphs, charts, tables and reports using data visualization methods for data-driven planning, decision-making, presentation and reporting.
Competencies
Professionalism:  Good research, analytical and problem-solving skills. Ability to identify and participate in the resolution of issues/problems.  Knowledge of various research methodologies and sources, including electronic sources on the internet, intranet and other databases. Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work. Able to analyze and interpret data in support of decision-making and convey resulting information to management.

Teamwork:  Works collaboratively with colleagues to achieve organizational goals; solicits input by genuinely valuing others’ ideas and expertise; is willing to learn from others; places team agenda before personal agenda; supports and acts in accordance with final group decision, even when such decisions may not entirely reflect own position; shares credit for team accomplishments and accepts joint responsibility for team shortcomings.

Planning& Organizing:  Develops clear goals that are consistent with agreed strategies; identifies priority activities and assignments; adjusts priorities as required; allocates appropriate amount of time and resources for completing work; foresees risks and allows for contingencies when planning; monitors and adjusts plans and actions as necessary; uses time efficiently.

 

Eligibility & Suitability Req
Education:    A first-level university degree is required, preferably in the field of sociology, economics, other social science or related field. Successful completion of both degree and non-degree programs in data analytics, business analytics or data science programs is an advantage. 

 

Experience:  A minimum of two years of progressively responsible experience in socio economic development, policy analysis, sociological research or related area is desirable. 1 year or more of experience in data analytics or related area is desirable (none if successful YPP or P-1 with a related degree). 

 

Language:  English and French are the two working languages of the United Nations. For this post, fluency in English is required. Knowledge of another UN official language is an advantage.

Org. Setting and Reporting
This position is located in the Economic Development Division (EDD). The Division contributes to sustainable and inclusive economic growth in Latin America and the Caribbean by strengthening the capacity of policymakers in the region to analyse current and emerging macroeconomic and development finance issues, and to evaluate, design and implement development-centred policies in these areas that incorporate a gender perspective and are based on comparative policy analysis. Please visit our website at https://www.cepal.org/en/work-areas/economic-development for further information.


The Economic Commission for Latin America and the Caribbean is one of the five regional Commissions of the United Nations. It was founded in 1948 with the purpose of contributing to the region's economic and social development. Its mission includes the design, monitoring and evaluation of public policies and the provision of advisory services, expertise and training to Governments, as well as support for regional and international cooperation and coordination activities. Please visit our website at www.eclac.org for further information.
 

Responsibilities
Within delegated authority, the duties of an Associate Economic Affairs Officer are likely to fall mainly into one of the three functional areas below:

ECONOMIC AND/OR SECTOR ANALYSIS:
•    Collects, evaluates, interprets, analyses specified statistics and qualitative socio-economic information obtained from a variety of sources and pertaining to particular sectors, countries, regions or development topics.
•    Maintains databases and programmes for database management and organizes nonquantitative information.
•    Assists in the design, testing, operation and modification of econometric models.
•    Prepares briefing notes, inputs to speeches and similar material on economic or sector issues in a form that is meaningful to non-specialists;
•    Prepares first drafts of sector, country or regional components of analytical studies on development issues and policies.
•    Assists in the organization and servicing of expert group meetings, seminars, etc on development issues.
•    Contributes to the elaboration of Divisional flagships, including the Economic Survey of Latin America and the Caribbean, the Preliminary Overview of the Economies of Latin America and the Caribbean, and the Fiscal Panorama of Latin America and the Caribbean.

INTERGOVERNMENTAL SUPPORT:
•    Prepares inputs for reports to intergovernmental bodies.
•    Follows intergovernmental meetings and prepares summary reports and similar material.
•    Prepares inputs to statements by members of the Bureau and Secretariat staff to such meetings.
•    Assists in the organization of panels, round tables on development issues for intergovernmental processes.

TECHNICAL COOPERATION:
•    Compiles and analyses the data and other information necessary for the preparation of technical cooperation project proposals in the area of economic development.
•    Prepares documentation for technical cooperation programmes and projects.
•    Contributes to the preparation of material for the approval of regional, country or sector technical cooperation projects.
•    Monitors, backstops and assesses the implementation of technical cooperation programmes and projects.

GENERAL:
•    Contributes to the collection and analysis of data as well as identification of trends or patterns and provides draft insights through graphs, charts, tables and reports using data visualization methods for data-driven planning, decision-making, presentation and reporting.
•    Performs other related duties as required, including a variety of administrative tasks necessary for the final delivery of the work unit's services;
•    Undertakes on-the-job and other training activities, both internally and externally.
 

Competencies
•    PROFESSIONALISM: Knowledge of economic theories and their application. Ability to identify and develop sources for data collection. Ability to undertake research, analyse data, make recommendations and write reports. The ability to analyze and interpret data in support of decision-making and convey resulting information to management. Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work.

•    COMMUNICATION: Speaks and writes clearly and effectively; listens to others, correctly interprets messages from others and responds appropriately; asks questions to clarify, and exhibits interest in having two-way communication; tailors language, tone, style and format to match audience; demonstrates openness in sharing information and keeping people informed.

•    PLANNING& ORGANIZING: Develops clear goals that are consistent with agreed strategies; identifies priority activities and assignments; adjusts priorities as required; allocates appropriate amount of time and resources for completing work; foresees risks and allows for contingencies when planning; monitors and adjusts plans and actions as necessary; uses time efficiently.
 

Eligibility & Suitability Req
Education: A first-level university degree is required; preferably in economics or related field is desirable.

Successful completion of both degree and non-degree programs in data analytics, business analytics or data science programs is desirable.

Experience: Experience in economic research and analysis, policy formulation, application of economic principles in development programmes or related area is desirable.

One year or more of experience in data analytics or related area is desirable.

Language: English and French are the two working languages of the United Nations. For this post, fluency in English is required. Knowledge of another UN official language is an advantage.

Org. Setting and Reporting
The Economic and Social Commission for Western Asia (ESCWA) forms part of the United Nations Secretariat and, like the other regional commissions, operates under the supervision of the United Nations Economic and Social Council. ESCWA comprises 21 Arab countries in Western Asia and North Africa. ESCWA provides a framework for the formulation and harmonisation of sectoral policies for member countries, a platform for congress and coordination, a home for expertise and knowledge, and an information observatory. ESCWA activities are coordinated with the divisions and main offices of the Headquarters of the United Nations, specialised agencies, and international and regional organizations, including the League of Arab States and its subsidiary bodies, and the Gulf Cooperation Council.

This position is located in the Gender Justice, Population and Inclusive Development Cluster within the Economic and Social Commission for Western Asia. The Associate Social Affairs Officer reports to the head of the Social Protection Team. 

Responsibilities
Within delegated authority, the Associate Social Affairs Officer will be responsible for the following duties: 

Social Analysis 

Carries out basic research on selected aspects of critical areas of concern related to socio-economic issues, social development programmes, etc. – especially in the context of social protection in the Arab region – to include collecting, analyzing and presenting social protection reforms and related statistical data and other information, like announcements of social protection reforms and policy changes on national news and social media of member states, gathered and automatically scraped from diverse sources, participating in the analysis, digest and publication of national experiences in social (protection) planning and policy development, etc. 
Contributes to the preparation of various written outputs, e.g. draft background papers, sections of reports and studies, inputs to publications like the annual digest of social protection reforms in the Arab region, etc. 
Develops and maintains reference/resource information on specific topics, policy-related issues, recent developments and trends in the field, etc.; liaises with government entities, researchers, etc. to obtain and provide data and information; responds to various inquiries and information requests internally and externally. 
Provides administrative and substantive support to expert group meetings, panel discussions, conferences, etc., to include proposing agenda topics, researching and proposing experts, handling logistics, etc. 
Analyzes progress and developments in the implementation of specific programmes; contributes to the development of mechanisms for monitoring programme implementation. 
Undertakes outreach activity in assigned areas, providing presentations and other inputs (e.g. articles, publications), and attending professional and NGO meetings. 
Maintains awareness of current social affairs and related issues, to include relevant political, policy, gender issues/considerations or other developments in specific sector, country or region concerned. 
Maintain and update key online data portals related to social protection reforms in the Arab region (i.e. Social Protection Platform in the Arab Region).
Intergovernmental Support 

Provides support to intergovernmental processes dealing with social development issues in general and in specific social protection by: preparing inputs for reports to intergovernmental bodies; following intergovernmental meetings and preparing summary reports and similar material; preparing inputs to statements by members of the Secretariat staff to such meetings; assisting in the organization of expert group meetings (e.g. Expert Group on Social Protection Reforms in the Arab region), panels, round tables, etc. on social development issues for intergovernmental processes. 
Technical Cooperation 

Compiles and analyses the data and other information necessary for the preparation of technical cooperation project proposals and implementation in the area of social development and specifically in the field of social protection. 
Prepares documentation for technical cooperation programmes and projects. 
Contributes to the preparation of material for the approval of regional, country or sectoral technical cooperation projects. 
Monitors, backstops and assesses the implementation of technical cooperation programmes and projects. 
Participates in fact-finding and advisory missions to government and other activities in support of work programme. 
General 

Performs other related duties as required, including a variety of administrative tasks necessary for the final delivery of the work unit’s services, e.g. on project administration (e.g. monitoring progress of deliverables of DA projects etc.) and co-financing activities and regularly contributes to the management of these administrative tasks and activities
Undertakes on-the-job and other training activities, both internally and externally. 
Contributes to the collection and analysis of data as well as identification of trends or patterns and provides draft insights through graphs, charts, tables and reports using data visualization methods for data-driven planning, decision-making, presentation and reporting. 
Work implies frequent interaction with the following: 
Counterparts in DESA and the Regional Commissions. 
Competencies
            Professionalism: 

Ability to identify and participate in the resolution of issues/problems. 
Knowledge of various research methodologies and sources, including electronic sources on the internet, intranet and other databases. 
Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. 
Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work. 
Ability to analyze and interpret data in support of decision-making and convey resulting information to management.

Org. Setting and Reporting
The Associate Economic Affairs Officer is responsible for conducting economic analysis and research, gathering data, promoting the organization’s positions, and supporting relationships with external stakeholders. The role engages with a wide range of partners throughout the research and policy process—collecting data, consulting on methodologies, disseminating findings, representing the organization, and strengthening collaborative relationships. This post is located in the Demographic Dynamics and Innovation-driven Development Section (DDIDS) in the Sub Regional Office – Western Africa and reports to the Chief of Section under the overall supervision by the Director of the Subregional Office. 

Responsibilities
Support the collection, integration, and analysis of data, contribute to identifying trends or patterns, and prepare draft insights through graphs, charts, tables, and reports using data visualization methods to support data‑driven planning, decision‑making, presentation, and reporting.
Analyze data on regional, sub-regional and national economic indicators, including macroeconomic indicators, using statistical tools in collaboration with internal and external research partners, to generate insights that inform policies and programme design.
Maintain and regularly update socio-economic databases by validating, organizing, and integrating new data to support timely analysis and inform the work of multiple organizational units.
Contribute to econometric and statistical analysis by supporting the interpretation of outputs and providing inputs to scenario planning, evidence-based decision-making, and programme design.
Contribute to cross-sectoral economic analysis and macroeconomic modeling by gathering data from statistics offices, and International Financial Institutions (IFIs), and other relevant data sources, conducting preliminary analysis and providing inputs to support economic planning.
Contribute to the monitoring and analysis of fiscal policy development by analyzing data on agreed indicators using appropriate quantitative methodologies and producing findings.
Contribute to the analysis of the structure and the cost of public debt based on different sources and provide initial findings for policy recommendations.
Contribute to the assessment of international trade and investment flows by analyzing cross-border data and trends and producing initial findings to support insights for regional and global economic cooperation.
Contribute to studies on regional integration, trade corridors, regional value chains, regional investment clusters, and cross-border linkages by analysis and synthesizing findings to support regional development initiatives and cooperation frameworks.
Contribute to the monitoring and assessment of the impact of tariff and non-tariff measures on trade flows and regional integration and provide findings.
Provide data analysis and advisory inputs on labour mobility and migration policies within and between countries by synthesizing evidence to promote inclusion and support regional cooperation.
Contribute to the analysis of industrial and innovation clusters by integrating data, literature, and stakeholder inputs to support regional growth through industry cooperation and new technologies, while also ensuring environmental protection.
Contribute to the development and implementation of models and frameworks that support economic planning and transformation in the context of climate resilience, nature-positive outcomes, pollution reduction, and sustainable natural resource management through conducting data analysis and generating analytical findings.
Contribute to the implementation, monitoring, and evaluation of programmes and projects on economic affairs by supporting coordination, tracking progress, and providing inputs to facilitate timely delivery.
Contribute to policy advisory missions by conducting initial analysis, preparing background materials, and supporting inclusive economic and development planning processes.
Support the integration of regional and sub-regional economic dimensions into national planning processes, institutional frameworks, and policy coordination mechanisms through research and technical assistance.
Contribute to the organization of technical meetings and stakeholder workshops by supporting agenda development, stakeholder coordination, and documentation of discussions to facilitate consensus-building and capture lessons learned.

Org. Setting and Reporting
The Economic and Social Commission for Western Asia (ESCWA) forms part of the United Nations Secretariat and, like the other regional commissions, operates under the supervision of the United Nations Economic and Social Council. ESCWA comprises 21 Arab countries in Western Asia and North Africa. ESCWA provides a framework for the formulation and harmonisation of sectoral policies for member countries, a platform for congress and coordination, a home for expertise and knowledge, and an information observatory. ESCWA activities are coordinated with the divisions and main offices of the Headquarters of the United Nations, specialised agencies, and international and regional organizations, including the League of Arab States and its subsidiary bodies, and the Gulf Cooperation Council. 

This position is in the External Relations and Communications Section (ERC). ERC is headed by the Secretary of the Commission and handles the Commission’s relationships with Member States and development partners, and communication with the media and the public. It covers the following three main areas of work: external relations, technical cooperation, and media and outreach. The Associate Programme Management Officer reports to the Secretary of the Commission.

Responsibilities

•    Supports the Secretary of the Commission in coordinating ESCWA’s engagement in regional and country-level United Nations processes; liaises with substantive divisions and relevant United Nations counterparts to consolidate institutional inputs, identify opportunities for collaboration and monitor follow-up on agreed actions.
•    Provides substantive and organizational support to ESCWA’s role in the Joint Secretariat of the Arab States Regional Collaborative Platform and evolving regional coordination arrangements; contributes to the preparation and monitoring of joint workplans, consolidation of inputs from participating entities and Issue-Based Coalitions, and preparation of system-wide results reports.

•    Reviews and consolidates ESCWA’s contributions to Common Country Analyses, United Nations Sustainable Development Cooperation Frameworks and related planning, monitoring and reporting processes; identifies opportunities to integrate regional perspectives, normative frameworks and ESCWA’s analytical and technical expertise.
•    Carries out research and analysis on emerging development trends, regional priorities and United Nations reform initiatives; collects and synthesizes information from diverse sources to support policy coordination, institutional positioning and senior management decision-making.
•    Contributes to the preparation and review of briefing notes, talking points, background papers, presentations, analytical reports and official correspondence for senior management; reviews inputs for accuracy, coherence and consistency with institutional priorities and relevant mandates.
•    Provides substantive and administrative support to high-level regional meetings, inter-agency consultations and intergovernmental processes, including the preparation of agendas, meeting documentation and presentations; coordinates inputs and logistical arrangements, prepares meeting records and tracks follow-up on conclusions and recommendations.
•    Supports partnerships and outreach with United Nations entities, government counterparts, academic institutions and other relevant stakeholders; assists in organizing consultations, expert group meetings and capacity-development activities, and contributes presentations and substantive inputs on assigned topics.
•    Participates in surveys, needs assessments and other data-collection initiatives supporting assigned programmes and country engagement; assists in developing data-collection tools, analyzes responses and prepares findings to inform programme design and implementation.
•    Contributes to the collection, analysis and presentation of data on ESCWA’s country-level engagement and programme results, including inputs to corporate and United Nations system reporting platforms; prepares charts, tables, dashboards and other visual outputs to support planning, reporting and communication of results.
•    Performs other duties as required.

Org. Setting and Reporting
The Economic and Social Commission for Western Asia (ESCWA) forms part of the United Nations Secretariat and, like the other regional commissions, operates under the supervision of the United Nations Economic and Social Council. ESCWA comprises 21 Arab countries in Western Asia and North Africa. ESCWA provides a framework for the formulation and harmonisation of sectoral policies for member countries, a platform for congress and coordination, a home for expertise and knowledge, and an information observatory. ESCWA activities are coordinated with the divisions and main offices of the Headquarters of the United Nations, specialised agencies, and international and regional organizations, including the League of Arab States and its subsidiary bodies, and the Gulf Cooperation Council.

This position is located in the Data Science and Decision-Support Division. The Data Science and Decision-Support Division (DSDSD) is part of ESCWA’s broader modernization and innovation efforts, providing advanced analytics and decision-support services within ESCWA, other UN entities, and Member States. Aligned with the UN 2.0 agenda and grounded in strategic foresight, DSDS leverages data-driven insights, emerging technologies, and scenario-based planning to anticipate trends and proactively inform policymaking and operations. Its core functions include data integration and management; data quality assurance; advanced analytics and modeling; machine learning and artificial intelligence solutions; real-time dashboards and performance reporting; and data visualization and business intelligence and the design and development of specialized digital decision-support tools. Through these capabilities, DSDS empowers evidence-based decision-making, fosters organizational efficiency, and catalyzes strategic innovation across the region, supporting sustainable development outcomes regionally and globally.

The Associate Information Systems Officer will report to the Chief of DSDS.
 

Responsibilities
Within delegated authority, the Associate Information Systems Officer will be responsible for the following duties: 

 

Participates in preparing user requirements and other technical specifications. 
Assists in the design, development and installation of information systems, including cloud-based applications, APIs, workflow automation and AI-enabled solutions, using modern software development practices.
 Undertakes analysis of well-defined modules within the system, develop enhancements and new features to existing systems; may design and implement small, stand-alone systems as needed (e.g. to meet individual user requirements).
Undertakes rigorous testing and proving of applications software, including automated and regression testing, and evaluates AI-enabled solutions for accuracy, reliability, performance, bias and security.
Maintains assigned portions of systems, providing operational support for system’s applications, analyzing and implementing system changes/upgrades, etc.
Participates in developing and maintaining Information Systems project plans, schedules, and budgets, applying Agile methodologies, iterative development and DevOps practices, as appropriate. Assists in identifying appropriate human and technical resources, monitoring project progress and risks, addressing deviations, escalating issues, preparing status reports and ensuring quality assurance of project deliverables.
Writes and develops programs to interface with existing systems.
Ensures data security and integrity, applying cybersecurity, privacy-by-design and data governance principles, including appropriate safeguards for AI-enabled systems, sensitive information and responsible AI use.
Participates in developing disaster recovery plans.
Prepares, updates and maintains system’s documentation and related technical/procedural manuals.
Assists in procurements, including conducting needs assessments and benchmarks, preparing technical specifications and evaluation criteria.
Assists in the set-up and monitoring of software performance measures.
Drafts training materials and conducts training sessions and demonstrations of systems for users.
Participates with senior staff in assessing, prototyping and testing emerging technologies, including generative AI, large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), prompt engineering, intelligent automation and AI-assisted development tools, evaluating their suitability, risks and potential organizational benefits.
Performs ad hoc duties as required.
May provide guidance to new/junior staff. 
Contributes to the collection, preparation, analysis and visualization of data; identifies trends and patterns and develops draft insights through interactive dashboards, charts, tables and reports, leveraging modern analytics platforms and, where appropriate, AI-assisted analytical tools to support evidence-based planning, decision-making and reporting.
Competencies
•    Professionalism:  Knowledge of at least two programming languages, basic systems analysis and design techniques, testing, debugging, and documentation standards, database design, storage and internal systems. Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work. Able to analyze and interpret data in support of decision-making and convey resulting information to management.
•    Communication:  Speaks and writes clearly and effectively; listens to others, correctly interprets messages from others and responds appropriately; asks questions to clarify, and exhibits interest in having two-way communication; tailors language, tone, style and format to match audience; demonstrates openness in sharing information and keeping people informed.
•    Planning & Organizing:  Develops clear goals that are consistent with agreed strategies; identifies priority activities and assignments; adjusts priorities as required; allocates appropriate amount of time and resources for completing work; foresees risks and allows for contingencies when planning; monitors and adjusts plans and actions as necessary; uses time efficiently.

Eligibility & Suitability Req
Education: 

A first-level university degree is required; preferably in computer science, information systems, artificial intelligence, cybersecurity, data science, or related field is desirable.

Experience:

•    Experience in the planning, design, development, implementation and maintenance of computer information systems or related area is desirable. 
•    Experience in developing and maintaining cloud-based applications and services is desirable.
•    Experience with modern software development practices, including Agile methodologies, DevOps and CI/CD pipelines, is desirable.
•    Experience in developing or integrating AI-enabled applications, including generative AI and large language models (LLMs), is desirable.
•    Experience with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), prompt engineering and AI-assisted development tools is desirable.
•    Experience in business process automation, workflow optimization and systems integration using APIs is desirable.
•    Experience in data analytics, data visualization and modern data management platforms is desirable.
•    Experience in applying cybersecurity, data privacy and responsible AI principles to information systems is desirable.

Language: 

English and French are the two working languages of the United Nations. For this post, fluency in English is required.

Org. Setting and Reporting
This position is located in the Web Services Unit, Statistics Division. The Associate Information Management Officer reports to the Director of the Division.


The Economic Commission for Latin America and the Caribbean is one of the five regional Commissions of the United Nations. It was founded in 1948 with the purpose of contributing to the region's economic and social development. Its mission includes the design, monitoring and evaluation of public policies and the provision of advisory services, expertise and training to Governments, as well as support for regional and international cooperation and coordination activities. Please visit our website at www.eclac.org for further information.

Responsibilities
Under the overall supervision and delegated authority of the Director of the Division, the Associate Information Management Officer will be responsible for the following duties:

•    Provides electronic record keeping support and advice to records-creating offices by advising on electronic record keeping system standards and compliance; oversees the digital records accessioning process; provides training in the use of metadata standards for the capture, maintenance and accessioning of digital archives; and manages system upgrades and migration for digital archives databases and web content.
•    Supervises and undertakes records accession and disposition functions, including records appraisal, by liasing with records-creating offices on the transfer and/or disposal of records and analysing and evaluating records for evidential and informational value in support of records appraisal.
•    Manages a portfolio of web and digital content management projects, establishes milestones, deliverables and deadlines in coordination with clients and follows up on their timely completion.
•    Supervises and undertakes archival processing activities by producing descriptive inventories; prepares mark-up of inventories for electronic dissemination; and monitors adherence to description, database and preservation standards.
•    Performs and supervises reference functions by advising internal and external users on holdings and accessibility and assisting in the administration of access and declassification activities.
•    Supervises General Service staff relating to functions cited above. 

•    Contributes to the collection and analysis of data as well as identification of trends or patterns and provides draft insights through graphs, charts, tables and reports using data visualization methods for data-driven planning, decision-making, presentation and reporting.
•    Performs other related duties, as required.

Competencies
•    Professionalism: Knowledge of information management and record keeping in electronic media. Ability to provide maintenance and disposition of records, including appraisal and migration management; web and digital content management; records preservation and description; and research practices. The ability to analyze and interpret data in support of decision-making and convey resulting information to management. Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work.

•    Planning& Organizing: Develops clear goals that are consistent with agreed strategies; identifies priority activities and assignments; adjusts priorities as required; allocates appropriate amount of time and resources for completing work; foresees risks and allows for contingencies when planning; monitors and adjusts plans and actions as necessary; uses time efficiently.

•    Client Orientation: Considers all those to whom services are provided to be "clients" and seeks to see things from clients' point of view; establishes and maintains productive partnerships with clients by gaining their trust and respect; identifies clients' needs and matches them to appropriate solutions; monitors ongoing developments inside and outside the clients' environment to keep informed and anticipate problems; keeps clients informed of progress or setbacks in projects; meets timeline for delivery of products or services to client.

Eligibility & Suitability Req
Education: A first-level university degree is required; preferably in archival, information science, information systems, social science or related field is desirable.

Certification in CMS project management or Drupal website development is desirable.

Successful completion of both degree and non-degree programs in data analytics, business analytics or data science programs is desirable.

Experience: Experience in modern archives management, record keeping, library, information management or related area is desirable.

Experience in managing enterprise CMS infrastructures (such as Drupal) is desirable.
    
One year or more of experience in data analytics or related area is desirable.

Org. Setting and Reporting
This position is located in the Travel Unit, Travel and Transportation Section in the Facilities and Commercial Activities Service, Division of Administration, Department of Operational Support, New York. The incumbent reports directly to the Chief of the Travel Unit.


The Department of Operational Support (DOS) provides operational support to UN Secretariat entities globally, including advisory, operational and transactional support services and, where needed, exercises delegated authority on behalf of clients. DOS supports the entire UN Secretariat, consisting of almost 100 entities located around the globe.


The Facilities and Commercial Activities Service (FCAS) operates the Headquarters complex, striving to provide effective and efficient planning, management, maintenance and operation for all existing physical facilities and assets at the UN Headquarters, New York. FCAS also supports office and conference facilities, property management, travel and transportation services, archives and records management, mail and pouch operations, catering, giftshop and the United Nations Postal Administration.


The Travel and Transportation Section provides travel and transportation services at UN Headquarters (travel, shipments management, UN travel documents (e UNLP & UN Certificates), visas and host country services, local transportation, materials management and UNHQ garage operations.
 

Responsibilities
Within delegated authority, the Associate Travel Officer will be responsible for the following duties:

(a) Travel Business and Systems Support

      i. Travel Business Support

Supports management in monitoring operational performance, including through the use of business intelligence reporting tools;
Liaises with process experts across business and functional areas to define, harmonize and align cross-functional business processes.
      ii. Systems Support

Defines business requirements and establishes priorities for system enhancements;
Ensures that system enhancements are developed in accordance with approved specifications;
Coordinates the testing of system enhancements across the global travel community.
(b) Training

Delivers training to Local Process Experts (LPEs) and end users;
Supports and provides advice on the development and revision of training materials;
Develops user aids and guidance documentation for business processes not covered in detail by Umoja training materials.
(c) Production Support

Provides timely responses to functional inquiries from end users and Local Process Experts (LPEs) across the Secretariat;
Provides intensive support to Local Process Experts, as required;
Contributes to the Community of Practice by responding to frequently asked questions and sharing knowledge and best practices.
(d) Continued Implementation of Umoja Travel in the Secretariat

Conducts change impact assessments by comparing current ("as-is") and future ("to-be") processes and analyzing the implications of change, including changes to roles and responsibilities;
Leads data conversion activities, including data collection, cleansing, enrichment and validation, for all required data types (e.g., master and transactional data) across implementation phases, including mock conversions, dress rehearsals and cutover activities;
Leads testing activities, including Product Integration Testing (PIT) and User Verification Testing (UVT), to validate Umoja travel solutions;
Conducts user access mapping and workflow design, taking into account delegation of authority, departmental responsibilities, user roles and functional skill requirements.
Competencies
Professionalism :
Knowledge of administrative, budgetary, financial and human resources policies and procedures. Ability to apply various United Nations administrative rules and regulations in work situations. Conceptual analytical and evaluative skills to conduct independent research and analysis. Ability to identify issues, formulate opinions, make conclusions and recommendations. Analytical skills to report, monitor and interpret large data sets and recommend an appropriate course of action to improve performance as necessary. Shows pride in work and in achievements; demonstrates professional competence and mastery of subject matter; is conscientious and efficient in meeting commitments, observing deadlines and achieving results; is motivated by professional rather than personal concerns; shows persistence when faced with difficult problems or challenges; remains calm in stressful situations. Takes responsibility for incorporating gender perspectives and ensuring the equal participation of women and men in all areas of work.

Teamwork:
Works collaboratively with colleagues to achieve organizational goals. Solicits input by genuinely valuing others' ideas and expertise; is willing to learn from others. Places team agenda before personal agenda. Supports and acts in accordance with final group decision, even when such decisions may not entirely reflect own position. Shares credit for team accomplishments and accepts joint responsibility for team shortcomings.

Technological awareness:
Keeps abreast of available technology. Understands applicability and limitations of technology to the work of the office. Actively seeks to apply technology to appropriate tasks. Shows willingness to learn new technology.

A first-level university degree is required, preferably in business administration, computer science, project management or related field is desirable. 


Work Experience

Org. Setting and Reporting
This position is located in the Office of the Director of the Division on International Trade and Commodities, in the United Nations Trade and Development (UNCTAD). The Associate Economics Affairs Officer reports to the Chief of the Office of the Director. 

The Division seeks to build consensus, strengthen capacity and promote partnerships for trade policy, trade negotiations, trade in goods and services and the creative economy, competition and consumer protection laws, and managing issues arising at the intersection of trade, the environment and climate change. It monitors trends and provides evidence-based analysis to make international trade an engine for sustainable development. The Division also aims to ensure the effective, qualitative and beneficial participation of women in international trade, strengthen development gains from the commodity economy, and address the trade and development challenges of commodity dependence. Click on the link to learn more about the work of DITC: https://unctad.org/about/organization/division-international-trade-and-commodities

Responsibilities
Within delegated authority, the duties of the Associate Economic Affairs Officer include:

Economic and/or sector analysis
•    Collects, evaluates, interprets, analyses specified statistics and qualitative socio-economic information obtained from a variety of sources and pertaining to particular sectors, countries, regions or development topics. 
•    Maintains databases and programmes for database management and organizes non-quantitative information, including with a view to report on the work of the division. 
•    Assists in the design, testing, operation and modification of econometric models.
•    Prepares briefing notes, inputs to speeches and similar material on economic or sector issues for the use of Director, for the Office of UNCTAD’s Secretary General and other relevant stakeholders, in a form that is adapted to the audience.  
•    Prepares first drafts of sector, country or regional components of analytical studies on development issues and policies.

Intergovernmental support
•    Prepares inputs for reports to intergovernmental bodies.
•    Follows intergovernmental meetings and prepares summary reports and similar material.
•    Prepares inputs to statements by members of Office of the Director and UNCTAD Secretariat staff to such meetings.
•    Assists in the organization of expert group meeting, panels, round tables etc. on development issues for inter-governmental processes.  


Technical cooperation
•    Compiles and analyses the data and other information necessary for the preparation of technical cooperation project proposals in the area of economic development.
•    Prepares documentation for technical cooperation programmes and projects.
•    Contributes to the preparation of material for the approval of regional, country or sector technical cooperation projects including by coordinating with divisional and external stakeholders.
•    Monitors, backstops and assesses the implementation of technical cooperation programmes and projects, including by coordinating divisional inputs to inform the Director and to feed into the organisation’s reporting processes.

General
•    Performs other related duties as required, including a variety of administrative tasks necessary for the final delivery of the work unit’s services.
•    Supports in the implementation of the division’s communications and fundraising strategies.
•    Supports the division’s clearance process of publications and research material. 
•    Supports the division’s Results Based Management (RBM) reporting effort in coordination with divisional RBM focal points.
•    Undertakes on-the-job and other training activities, both internally and externally.
